## Review repair log: Iteration 2

Sources: `architecture-review.md` (re-review, BLOCKED on N1) and `adversarial-review.md` (round 2, BLOCKED on N1). Owner Decisions in `requirements.md` are unchanged and binding (the one refinement, ranking `unknown` last among non-terminal PRs in `ActivePR`, is recorded in the Glossary and ADR-002 and does not change the owner's order for `open`/terminal PRs). Plan documents only; no code was touched. Code claims re-checked are in the "Repair pass 2" spot-verification rows.

### Blockers

| # | Item | Resolution | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| Arch N1 | T5.1.3's auto-created item-level mirror link survives `AbandonPRs`, so round-1 PR stays live, item sticks in `pr_pending` | Verified against the code that `ItemSession` has no supersede marker and `AbandonPRs` only touched the session's own links. Remedy: `SessionPR.origin_session_uuid` set on mirror links; `AbandonPRs`/`AbandonByOrigin` abandons the superseded sessions' links AND item links with that origin; item-owned links (empty origin) are never abandoned by a supersede; all-links rule defined (a state is abandoned for an item only if EVERY reachable link is abandoned) and used by the verdict, gate and sweeper; AC for the exact case (mirror link, round superseded, `Done` once #31 merges) plus the item-owned counter-case | Glossary (`SessionPR`, `abandoned`), T1.3.1, T1.3.2, T1.2.3, T5.1.2, T5.1.3, Story 5.1 AC, ADR-001, ADR-003 |
| Adv N1 | `PRRef` case-sensitivity: `Acme/App#12` and `acme/app#12` become two state rows | Verified `NewRepoRefWithHost` stores verbatim and `repoKey()` lowercases. `NewPRRef` canonicalizes host, owner and repo to lowercase and is the only constructor used on write and lookup paths (backfill, tombstones, discovery, scan, webhook, filters, search); display case lives only in `url`; Story 1.2 and 1.3 ACs; new test task T1.3.6 (all entry points plus a composite-literal guard test) | Glossary (`PRRef`), T1.2.1, T1.3.1, T1.3.2, T1.3.6, Stories 1.2 and 1.3, ADR-001 |

### Concerns (adversarial C1-C9, architecture concerns)

| Concern | Decision and rationale |
|---|---|
| C1 backfill seed uses a stored status that does not exist | Adopted. Verified the schema has only the terminal bool. Seed table: terminal session -> `unknown`, `terminal=true`, `outcome_unverified=true` (gate treats as terminal so history is not newly blocked; UI says "Closed or merged (unverified)"; poller verifies once); non-terminal -> `unknown`, `terminal=false`; items by status, `done` seeded like terminal sessions, `pr_pending` is `unknown` not `open`. The manually-completed-item-with-open-PR gap is stated and accepted. New column `outcome_unverified` (T1.3.1, proto field in T4.1.1) |
| C2 flag-off leak (gate/sweeper not gated, nothing refreshes rows) | Adopted option 1: state-row refresh runs always (T3.2.5, now M, "flag-independent state refresher") plus legacy merge detection writes the terminal row. Rejected "gate the gate": a second untested path on a data-loss site. Tested with all scopes off; bounded by ETag, `last_polled_at` skip and the cold cap |
| C3 gate at top of `CleanupTerminalItem` would skip archival | Adopted: gate only the worktree step; `archiveItemWorkSessions` unchanged and asserted by an AC (T5.4.2, Story 5.4 design and AC) |
| C4 "do not settle when blocked" needs an interface change; metric re-increments each tick | Adopted: `WorktreeCleaner.CleanupTerminalItem` returns `CleanupOutcome`; interface, implementation and all callers updated in T5.4.2; settle only on `Done`; counter dedupes per first block, gauge shows current blocked count; AC for the two-tick case |
| C5 path inventory covered only backlog removal | Adopted: scope stated; rows 9-13 added and classified (retention `DeleteSession` gated by T4.3.1; explicit user destroy/pause, MCP pause/destroy, diagnose and start-failure rollback intentionally ungated with reasons); new T5.4.5 makes explicit removals warn about open PRs; owner question recorded for the agent-callable MCP path. `session_service_diagnose.go` and the rollback sites were not opened (INFERRED) and T5.4.5 confirms |
| C6 "not superseded" has no backing field | Adopted: derived predicate (`ended_at` set and a later work/rework/review `ItemSession` exists), no new column; `AbandonPRs` demoted to an eager optimization; T5.1.4 enumerates every `ended_at` writer path (verified two writers, `storage_backlog.go:592, 612`) with one test per path |
| C7 abandoned per link vs verdict per PR; reused branch | Adopted: all-links rule in glossary, `ItemPRVerdict`, T5.1.2 dedup, gate; verdict AC for `{abandoned, non-abandoned}` to one open state and the reused-branch case |
| C8 `unknown` item-only PR has no exit; no backoff; ActivePR ranking | Adopted: item-scoped `DetachItemPullRequest`/`WaiveItemPullRequest` (new T4.2.7) with item-keyed tombstone; exponential backoff on `poll_fail_count` (cap 6h) plus cold cap; `ActivePR` ranks `unknown` below every `open` PR (AC) |
| C9 rollback + re-deploy loses data, reconciler overwrites | Adopted both: dropped the run-once marker for an idempotent per-boot zero-link top-up; reconciler imports unrepresented legacy PRs first and never overwrites a non-represented or lesser legacy value; new T1.5.3 rollback test and AC |
| Arch: poll result = one state write + N mirror writes, no transaction | Adopted: stated as accepted gap in T3.2.2 doc comment and Migration Plan; reconciler covers it; persists serialized per session (T2.3.1) |
| Arch: attach before session row exists; empty uuid at runtime | Adopted: `ErrSessionNotPersisted` defers the DB write to the first full save (carries snapshot PRs); `ErrNoSessionUUID` falls back to the legacy write, protected by the "legacy number, no link" rule (T2.3.1, Story 2.1 AC) |
| Arch: poller eligibility bound | Adopted: `ListPollableStates` returns only states with a non-abandoned link and a live owner (T1.3.2, T3.2.1, AC) |
| Arch: tombstones never collected | Adopted: deleted with the session/item and by the nightly GC (T1.3.5) |
| Arch: T4.3.1 (b)/(c) wording | Adopted: ordered decision list, legacy-flag check first, `PRsAllTerminal` only with links, tested in that order |
| Arch: `pr-field-guard` is receiver-name based | Adopted as non-blocking follow-up T2.2.6 (typed analyzer, precedent `noarchivedrevival`); limitation stated in T2.2.4 |
| Arch: eight new files in `session` | Accepted as in the review (no change); leaf-package split noted as debt, not done here |

|---|---|

## Review repair log: Iteration 3 (simplification)

Trigger: two patch rounds each produced new blockers in the same subsystem (item-level mirror links plus several writers to `PullRequestState`). Lesson applied: repeated blockers in one subsystem mean delete, not patch. Sources: `architecture-review.md` (BLOCKED N2, concerns C-a..C-c) and `adversarial-review.md` (BLOCKED N2, N3, concerns C1-C9, minors M1-M7). Owner Decisions in `requirements.md` are unchanged and binding. Plan documents only; no code touched. Entries in the Iteration 1 and 2 logs above that mention `abandoned`, `origin_session_uuid`, `AbandonPRs`, T5.1.3 mirror links, the derived `ended_at` predicate or waive RPCs describe machinery that iteration 3 DELETED; this section supersedes them.

### Deleted (and why it was safe)

| Deleted | Replaced by | Evidence it works |
|---|---|---|
| Materialized item-level "mirror" links (old T5.1.3), `SessionPR.origin_session_uuid`, `SessionPR.abandoned`, `AbandonPRs`/`AbandonByOrigin`/`SetAbandoned`, the all-links rule | A READ-TIME item PR set: item-direct links UNION the links of the item's current round work session, minus item tombstones, deduplicated by state id (Glossary: Item PR set, Current round; T5.1.2) | A mirror and a genuine item link shared one row, so promotion/demotion rules multiplied (Arch N2). With derivation there is one row per fact; waive/detach delete exactly that link, and a superseded session simply stops being the latest work session |
| Derived "superseded" predicate over `ended_at`/`started_at`/role including a `rework` role, and the supersede-path enumeration (old T5.1.4) | "Current round = latest `work` ItemSession by `created_at`, tie-break `id`" | `rework` does not exist and `started_at` is nullable (`session/backlog.go:50-64`, `item_session.go:35-37`); `created_at` is non-null and immutable (`item_session.go:112-114`); the rework create-then-cleanup order is VERIFIED (`backlog_service_triage.go:1261, 1294`) |
| `PullRequestState.outcome_unverified` column | "Unverified terminal" = `state=unknown` with `terminal=true` (adapter derives the proto/UI flag) | One fewer column and invariant; the poller work list and `ActivePR` already branch on `terminal` |
| `WaivePullRequest`, `WaiveItemPullRequest` RPCs | `DetachSessionPullRequest` / `DetachItemPullRequest` are the one exit | The only thing waive did beyond detach was set `abandoned`, which no longer exists |
| Two gate forms (`ItemAllowsCleanup`, `SessionsAllowCleanup`) and a pre-check inside `CleanupTerminalItem` | One call of `PRCleanupGate.Blocked(sessions)` at the top of `cleanupItemWorktreesExcept` | VERIFIED all backlog removal paths funnel there (spot-verification rows) |
| Legacy merge-detection paths writing the state row, and the `last_polled_at` skip rule (T3.2.5) | The refresher alone writes state; it picks up merges within one interval | Removes a second state writer |
| Reconciler import step and "direction rule" (T1.5.2) | The boot backfill imports unrepresented legacy numbers before `LoadInstances`; the reconciler only corrects drift | Removes the load-time projection hazard (C1) and the tombstoned stuck case (C8) |
| `ListPollableStates` eligibility bound ("non-abandoned link with a live owner") | Nightly GC only (T1.3.5) | Orphans cost at most one ETag 304 per interval until the GC |
| Composite-literal guard test (T1.3.6 part) | `PRRef` with unexported fields | Compile-time, not grep-time (C-b, M1) |

### Added (all small)

- Invariant I1 (single writer of `PullRequestState` columns): `UpdatePullRequestState` only from the poll fetch core; `EnsureState` (insert-if-absent) for attach, backfill and full saves. New T2.3.4 plus Story 2.3 AC `TestFullSave_DoesNotOverwritePolledState` ("seeded unknown+terminal polled to open stays open after a full save", including stale snapshots of suspended and archived sessions) and a caller allow-list test.
- Backfill: candidates = sessions whose legacy `PRRef` is not represented by their own links (covers rollback), per-pass `map[PRRef]seed` with non-terminal-wins merging.
- `ListPollableStates` ordering (non-terminal first, unverified terminal after, capped at 5 failed tries, no notification).
- Detach tombstone written in the same transaction as the link delete; item tombstone doubles as a read-time filter.
- Gate reason names each holding owner; `CleanupOutcome` distinguishes `Blocked` (next tick) from `Failed` (settle TTL); blocked-log dedupe.
- ent validators rejecting upper-case identity columns.

### Dispositions

| Item | Disposition |
|---|---|
| Arch N2 / Adv N3 (mirror and item-owned link share a row; waive/detach cannot release) | Eliminated by design: no mirror links, no `abandoned`/origin columns. Detach on a session link or an item link deletes exactly that link (+ its owner-keyed tombstone, one transaction); nothing else keeps the state "live". Story 5.1 AC covers both owners |
| Adv N2 (multiple state writers, no precedence) | Invariant I1, AC, test task T2.3.4 (see Added) |
| C1 buildSnapshot projects from `ActivePR` before the reconciler imports a rollback PR | Fixed: import moved into the boot backfill, which runs before `LoadInstances`; T1.5.3 now runs backfill -> `LoadInstances` -> full save |
| C2 predicate ignores role / `ended_at` on many paths / `rework` role absent / nullable `started_at` | Fixed by replacement: latest `work` session by `created_at`; `ended_at` and `started_at` not read; a later review never supersedes its work session (AC) |
| C3 all-abandoned states never polled, so merged-abandoned PRs cannot count | Moot: no abandoned state; superseded rounds' states keep being polled. Stated decision instead: a superseded round's PR does not count toward the verdict (owner confirm, Unresolved Questions) |
| C4 backfill merge rule for a shared `PRRef`; verdict for unverified rows | Fixed: non-terminal seed wins regardless of order; unverified terminal = terminal, never merged; a `pr_pending` item cannot see one (AC, Glossary) |
| C5 archive RPC calls `cleanupItemWorktrees` directly, item-owned open PR invisible | Fixed: gate at `cleanupItemWorktreesExcept` reads the item's direct links too; AC for an item-direct PR on path 1 |
| C6 unverified-terminal rows compete with blocking unknowns; notification storm | Fixed: work-list ordering, 5-try cap, no notification for terminal rows (Story 3.2 ACs) |
| C7 `ActivePR` ranks seeded unverified terminal above verified terminal | Fixed: rank by effective terminal then recency (Story 1.2 AC) |
| C8 reconciler direction rule stuck on tombstoned values | Moot: no direction rule; a tombstoned or unrepresented value is overwritten with the projection (Story 1.5 AC) |
| C9 does the legacy loop write `last_polled_at` (double conditional GET) | Resolved by stating the truth: it does not; no skip rule exists; one extra conditional GET (304) per PR per interval is accepted and pinned by a `<= 2` call-count AC |
| C-a (same as C9) | As C9 |
| C-b PRRef exported fields | Fixed: unexported fields + accessors, ent validator; literal-guard test deleted |
| C-c receiver-name guard | Unchanged: tracked by T2.2.6 (non-blocking) |
| Minors | M1/M2 done (above). M3 (closed PR reopened on GitHub stays terminal): accepted, same as legacy. M4 done (log dedupe). M5 done (runbook note in Migration Plan). M6 done (`Blocked` vs `Failed`). M7 done (tombstone + delete in one transaction) |

### Counts after iteration 3
11 epics, 30 stories, 101 tasks (T5.1.3 mirror task and T5.1.4 deleted, T5.1.3 reused for round-membership tests, T2.3.4 added; T3.2.5 M to S, T1.5.2 S to XS, T1.3.6 S to XS). Raw 8,415k tokens, 7,577k CU, about 13,254k CU with verification (was 13,601k); still Large (3-15M).

### Residual notes
- The session-row column `github_pr_status_terminal` still has two writers with the flag off (the legacy title-keyed narrow writer and full saves). Not new in iteration 3. Neither the gate nor the sweeper reads it for a session that has links (they read state rows), only for "legacy number, no link" sessions, where no full-save derivation applies.
- Two behavior changes versus iteration 2 need an owner yes/no (Unresolved Questions): an open PR on a superseded round keeps that session's worktree and retention; a superseded round's PR is ignored by the done verdict.

## Review repair log: Iteration 4 (targeted)

Trigger: architecture review round 4 (BLOCKED: N3 + C1-C7 + 2 nitpicks) and adversarial review round 4 (CONCERNS: C1-C8, M1-M6). Rule applied: smallest fix per item; text, AC and ordering changes first. Owner Decisions in `requirements.md` unchanged. Plan documents only; no code touched. Invariant I1 and fail-closed behavior at data-loss sites kept; no mirror links, `abandoned` flag or origin column reintroduced. Code claims below were checked by opening: `pr_status_poller.go:312-330` (fork skip at :322), `session_driver.go:950-967` (flag untouched), `session_retention_sweeper.go:118-150`, `backlog_service_triage.go:1334-1366`, `git grep StopSessionByUUID`, `session/ent/schema/session.go:150-157` (empty host = github.com; no fork column).

### New machinery (and why nothing smaller works)

Exactly one: a nullable `PullRequestState.first_fail_at` column (T1.3.1). The "5 failures spanning >= 30 minutes" rule already existed in T3.2.4 with no stored basis; a count alone cannot prove a time span (the exponential backoff of 1,2,4,8 ticks can fit inside the span at short tick intervals). Everything else is predicates, ACs, one Makefile grep ratchet in the existing `pr-field-guard` style, one `Storage` sibling query (`ListItemDirectPRViewsByItemIDs`, same shape as T4.3.0), and two `InstanceData` booleans computed at load.

### Dispositions

| Item | Disposition |
|---|---|
| Arch N3 (BLOCKER) backfill re-creates a permanent item-direct link | Fixed, stricter than the suggested rule: item candidate = `pr_number > 0` and the ref is not represented by ANY link of the item or of any of its sessions (every round), tombstones excluded. "Not in the derived set" alone would still re-import a SUPERSEDED round's PR that the mirror still holds. Migration Plan, T1.5.1, Story 1.5 AC. `itemPRs` legacy fallback uses the same helper (Story 5.1 AC, T5.1.2). Mirror recompute timing specified in T5.2.3 and Story 5.2: in the writer's statement for item-direct changes, at the end of each `ReconcilePRPending` pass for session-link changes, never zeroed by an empty set. ACs: backfill twice with a session-only PR creates nothing, then a later round supersedes it |
| Adv C2 (DATA LOSS) sweeper ignores item-direct links | Fixed: T4.3.1 step 4 consults the item-direct links of the session's item (one `ListItemSessionsBySessionUUIDs`, one new bulk item query), fail closed on error; inventory row 7 updated; Story 4.3 AC with the test written first. ADR-003 updated |
| Adv C1 `stopLiveWorkAndReviewSessions` unlisted | Row 14 added, VERIFIED callers; classified intentionally ungated (explicit operator reset, dirty-worktree refusal remains, branch and PR survive) with a visible warning in T5.4.5 and an owner confirm. Headline claim narrowed to "no AUTOMATED path" |
| Adv C4 current round can hide a live PR | Current round = latest work session THAT HAS A LINK; deterministic tie-break stated as non-semantic. Glossary, ADR-001/003, T5.1.2, ACs for attach-before-discovery and rework-before-discovery |
| Adv C3 ACs only cover session-only links | Added item-direct first-PR-then-rework ACs and stated the outcome (item-direct PRs are never superseded; the item waits for terminal or detach; stuck report names it). Owner question rewritten to cover both halves |
| Adv C5 + Arch C2 monotonic writer | `UpdatePullRequestState`: CAS on `observed_at`, merged/closed sticky, exceptions only for unverified-terminal resolution and a webhook-proven reopen (`source=webhook_reopened`, strictly later, successful `open`). Story 1.3 AC, T1.3.2, T3.3.2 |
| Adv C6 backfill failure handling | Errors abort startup like other migrations [SUPERSEDED by Phase 4 pre-mortem #1: transient errors are retried 3 times and the service keeps booting] (the template swallowing errors is deliberately not copied); `InstanceData.LegacyPRUnrepresented` stops full saves and the reconciler from overwriting a skipped class; column-narrow candidate query and capped skip log; Story 1.5 AC |
| Adv C7 stale terminal flag seeds a new PR terminal | Legacy writer clears the flag when it changes the number (T2.2.1, first step); seeded unverified terminal rows count as terminal only once `VerifyExhausted()`; Glossary, seed table, Stories 1.2, 1.5, 4.3 ACs updated. Changes the iteration 3 stance "historical sessions are not newly blocked": they are blocked for at most the cold drain (new owner-visible consequence, in Needs the owner) |
| Adv C8 5-try cap | Counted classes only 404/401/403, span >= 30 min via `first_fail_at`, counters reset at boot (T3.2.5) and nightly (T1.3.5 note via T3.2.5) [SUPERSEDED: iteration 5 removed the nightly reset; do not implement it], gauge `ssq_pr_unverified_terminal_rows`; Story 3.2 AC |
| Arch C1 flag-off terminal source of truth | Glossary "Session terminal": `buildSnapshot` copies the Instance field; legacy loop is the only deriver with scopes off; flag-off two-link characterization test (T3.2.5) |
| Arch C3 single-writer guard scope | Allow-list covers the `Storage` wrapper; new `pr-state-write-guard` grep ratchet for direct ent-client writes (T2.3.4, S to M); typed analyzer stays the T2.2.6-style follow-up |
| Arch C4 `PRRef{}` and JSON | `IsZero()`, `ErrZeroPRRef` at every entry point, `String()`/`MarshalText`; test in T1.3.6 and Story 1.3 AC |
| Arch C5 gate vs sweeper "legacy number, no link" | One shared helper `LegacyOnlyPRSafe(d)`; gate clause (iii) now agrees with sweeper step 2; Story 5.4 AC |
| Arch C6 fork sessions | Fork flag is not an ent column (VERIFIED), so the backfill cannot see it. Live `AttachPR` skips forks (legacy scalar path); an already-linked fork session goes `unknown` after exhaustion and the exit is session detach; documented as accepted/rare (T3.2.5). Item detach reachable for archived/done items: T6.2.5 AC |
| Arch C7 `ReconcilePRPending` two truth sources | Reconciler observation is a TRIGGER (`InvalidateAndRefresh`), never an overlay on the verdict; one-tick lag accepted and pinned by a test (T5.2.1) |
| M1 | Rollback paragraph states reverting accepts today's behavior |

| M3 | `requirements.md` Success Metrics line aligned to the owner's done rule; ADR-003 records the narrowing |
| M4, M5 | T5.2.2: stuck report lists blocked-cleanup done/archived items with age, and names both detaches |
| M6 | T1.3.2: full saves always `EnsureState(unknown, terminal=false)` |
| Nitpicks | Runbook lines added to T11.1.1 (abandoned superseded PR retains the worktree; reopen path 5 keeps prior worktrees) |

### Counts after iteration 4
11 epics, 30 stories, 101 tasks (no task added or removed; T2.3.4 S to M, T5.1.3 S to M). Glossary 28 terms (rows edited, none added). Raw 8,565k tokens, 7,711k CU, about 13,490k CU with verification (was 13,254k); still Large (3-15M). Waves unchanged (6); added ordering notes: `pr-state-write-guard` after `pr-field-guard` in the Makefile, T4.3.0 sibling before T4.3.1 and T5.4.1, the unrepresented-legacy-item-number helper defined in T1.5.1 and imported by T5.1.2.

### Needs the owner
- Iteration 4 changes the historical-session stance: a backfill-seeded "unverified terminal" PR no longer lets the gate or sweeper act until a poll resolves it or verification is exhausted (5 counted failures over >= 30 minutes); expect a short post-boot window where old done/archived items keep their worktree. Confirm.
- Item-direct PRs are never superseded (a different round-2 PR does not release an open round-1 agent-reported PR); confirm or ask for a rule.
- Force-reset / backward-to-ready stays ungated with a warning (row 14).
- Reopen acceptance: a PR reopened on GitHub is picked up only via a `pull_request.reopened` webhook; otherwise a merged/closed row stays terminal (same as legacy).

### Unresolved
- `GitHubIsFork` is not persisted as a column, so pre-existing fork sessions cannot be excluded at backfill; accepted with a detach exit.
- (Iteration 5: the earlier note here, calling the T1.5.2/T1.5.3 wave-0 placement a "pre-existing, not new" tension, was wrong and is withdrawn. It was a real ordering violation, fixed in iteration 5 item 1.)

Trigger: architecture review round 5 (BLOCKED: B1 + C1-C7 + 2 nitpicks) and adversarial review round 5 (CONCERNS: C1-C6, M1-M5). |---|---|
| 1. Arch B1 (BLOCKER) T1.5.2/T1.5.3 in wave 0 need E2 | Fixed. T1.5.1 stays wave 0 (needs only E1 queries). T1.5.2, T1.5.3 and the Story 1.5 ACs that load instances, full-save or run the reconciler (marked **[wave 1]**) moved to the tail of wave 1 after T2.3.3; wave table, dependency diagram, critical path updated; the old "pre-existing, not new" note withdrawn. Cycle re-check written into the diagram: E1[T1.5.1] -> E2[T2.1.1 -> T2.1.2 -> T2.2.x; T2.3.1 -> T2.3.2 -> T2.3.3 -> T2.3.4] -> {T1.5.2 -> T1.5.3}; no E1 task needs E2 code; T2.2.1 no longer needs T2.3.3 (it uses the existing title-keyed writer) |
| 2. Arch C1 tombstone scope | "Tombstones excluded" = item tombstone OR a tombstone of any of its sessions, in T1.5.1, T5.1.2 and T5.2.3 (one shared helper). Fallback short-circuits when `pr_number` matches a ref in the derived set. ACs in Story 1.5 and 5.1. `DeleteSession` consequence recorded as K9 |
| 3. Arch C2 / Adv C2 flags and stale terminal | `LegacyPRUnrepresented` and `HasPRLinks` are `Instance` fields carried in `InstanceSnapshot` and out through `ToInstanceData`, set at load, recomputed by `AttachPR`/`DetachPR` (T2.1.1, T2.1.2, T2.3.2); test: load -> save -> load -> save keeps the legacy columns. Stale terminal flag for link-less sessions (fork, empty uuid): the legacy writer clears it when the number changes regardless of links AND persists the clear at once via the existing title-keyed `UpdateGitHubPRStatusTerminal` (works without a uuid or link), with a `terminalClearPending` retry bit; AC through the real save/reload path |
| 4. Arch C3 / Adv C1 boot loop | Errors classified: transient (query/begin/commit/busy/I/O/context, and any unrecognized type) abort [SUPERSEDED by Phase 4 pre-mortem #1: retried 3 times, then boot continues]; deterministic per-row failures (validator, `ErrZeroPRRef`, link cap, primary index, parse failure) are a counted skip class with count + first 10 titles, rolled back per candidate; the fail-closed clauses cover skipped sessions (`LegacyPRUnrepresented`/`LegacyOnlyPRSafe`) and skipped items (gate clause iii, sweeper step 4). Item `PRRef` = parse of `pr_url` (`/{owner}/{repo}/pull/{n}`, number must equal `pr_number`), no repo_path fallback because the code has none. T1.5.1 "best-effort per row" contradiction removed. Rollback import role is `followup` when a primary exists. `Restart=on-failure` noted. Poisoned-row AC added |
| 5. Arch C4 / Adv C4 counter resets | Nightly reset removed. Boot reset is the named `ResetVerifyCounters()` in `ent_repository_session_pr.go`, listed as the one I1 exception, run inside the startup migration before any poller/sweeper/ticker exists (so no CAS race and the ordering AC holds by construction). Exhaustion span = `first_fail_at` to the LAST counted failure (`last_polled_at`); only counted failures write the failure columns, so time or a 5xx cannot make a row terminal. Allow-list tests (caller list, `pr-state-write-guard`) updated; GC is delete-only |
| 6. Arch C5 transition table | Transition table added to T1.3.2 (open->merged/closed; closed->merged any later source; closed->open only `webhook_reopened`; merged sticky; unknown->any; unverified->any; open->unknown only by the failure rule). `last_polled_at` = applied `observed_at`. Reconciler refresh (`source=reconciler_refresh`) fetches a `closed` row once per pass for an item that would be `AllClosedUnmerged` and applies only `closed->merged`, so today's closed-then-merged detection is kept (T5.2.1 test) |
| 7. Arch C7 `LegacyOnlyPRSafe` home | Defined in T4.3.0 (`session/pr_legacy_safe.go`); T4.3.1 and T5.4.1 import it and run in parallel |
| 8. Adv C5 counted-failure definition | Classifier in T3.2.4 with four tested cases: secondary rate limit / `Retry-After` / remaining 0 / 429 not counted; 401 or missing token counted only if the host succeeded in the last 15 minutes (token-wide failures not counted, host `auth` condition logged); 404/403 counted only when the repo-metadata GET succeeds (private/SSO/inaccessible not counted); 5xx/network never. T3.2.4 resized S to M |
| 9. Adv C6 current round after detach | Rule: walk work sessions newest first; skip only a session that has NEVER linked (no link and no session tombstone); a session with a tombstone but no link is the round and contributes nothing, so a detach cannot resurrect an earlier round; C4 guard retained. AC for the detach direction; delete direction is Known Risk K7 (DeleteSession removes tombstones) with a log signal |
| 10. Adv minors | M1: `ListItemIDsBySessionUUIDs` (all items per uuid) added to T4.3.0, sweeper step 4 and gate use it (VERIFIED the old call keeps only the newest row). M2: clamp for future `last_polled_at`, drop counter `ssq_pr_state_observation_dropped_total{reason}`, warn after 3 consecutive drops (K8). M3: one GC rule (owner gone AND older than 24h; states via one conditional `NOT EXISTS` statement; `created_at` column added to the new table). M4: the verdict reads an exhausted unverified row as held `unknown`, only gate/sweeper treat it as terminal (Story 1.2 ACs, Glossary, ADR-003). M5: runbook note on restarts |
| 11. Nitpicks and Adv C3 | Arch nitpicks: log line corrected; fallback short-circuit added. Adv C3 (reopen without webhook): recorded as accepted risk K1 plus runbook note; no polling added |

### Known Risks accepted
See the "Known Risks (accepted)" section (K1-K9) before Unresolved Questions: reopen-without-webhook (K1), unverified-terminal window and restart reset (K2), item-direct PRs never superseded (K3), ungated force-reset (K4), inaccessible-repo rows never exhaust (K5), skipped item with no parseable ref retains its worktree (K6), delete of the latest work session lets an earlier round re-enter (K7), CAS drops / clock step (K8), swept-session tombstones re-import a stale `pr_number` (K9). Owner for all: Tyler; each has a detection signal and an exit in the table.

### Counts after iteration 5
11 epics, 30 stories, 101 tasks (none added or removed; T3.2.4 S to M). Glossary 28 terms (rows edited, none added). Raw 8,640k tokens, 7,778k CU, about 13,609k CU with verification (was 13,490k); still Large (3-15M). Waves unchanged at six; T1.5.2/T1.5.3 now sit at the end of wave 1. New schema surface: one `created_at` column on `pull_request_state` (GC age basis); new repository/storage methods: `ResetVerifyCounters`, `ListItemIDsBySessionUUIDs`, a bulk session-tombstone read; one pure helper file `session/pr_legacy_safe.go`.

### Needs the owner
- Acknowledge Known Risks K1-K9 (K1-K4 are the owner's own accepted decisions; K5-K9 are new consequences of the iteration 5 fixes, all in the safe direction except K1, K4 and K7).
- Confirm that a `done`/`archived` item with an unparseable `pr_url` retains its worktree until a valid URL is set (K6).

### Remaining risks
- The hook-process claim (a non-service process cannot overwrite an unimported legacy number) rests on T2.3.3's set-only behavior for link-less sessions plus the architecture review's caller list; the hook save paths were not traced (marked INFERRED in the Migration Plan).
- Deferred backfill (cap) and the awaited refresh add two tunables (2,000 writes, 10s) with no soak data; both fail in the safe direction.
- A refresh that returns `open` for a closed row holds the item in `pr_pending` until the reopen webhook or a manual action (K1 behavior, now explicit).
- With item-direct links always written, a replaced PR number is unlinked only by the correction path's delete; a stale item-direct link created some other way (Jules re-poll, manual attach) of a wrong PR holds cleanup until it is detached (K3 behavior).

### Needs the owner
- Nothing new blocks implementation. Please confirm the two small tunables (2,000 writes per boot, 10s awaited refresh) or name different values.

### Owner request C: issue-close on re-import (Story 5.5)

Added to requirements.md (Success Metric 5 and Scope), plan Story 5.5 (T5.5.1-T5.5.4), ux.md S11 (desktop dialog and 390px bottom sheet, exit paths and error states, UX-S11-1..4), validation (U-136..U-138, I-111..I-116, UX-S11 rows, M-6) and ADR-003. Verified: `ImportGitHubIssue` has `issue.State` in hand and the existing-item branch at `backlog_service_sync.go:283-295`; `ImportGitHubIssueResponse` uses fields 1-4 so `issue_close_offer = 5` is free (re-confirm at `make proto-gen`; additive, `make proto-breaking`); the close reuses `CloseIssue`, `forwardSyncCloseCommentWithPR` and `ReadIssueState` skip-if-closed through an extracted shared core; the new `CloseFinishedItemIssue` RPC is classified in `notTerminalWriteRPCs` (it writes GitHub, never a pane; `ImportGitHubIssue` already sits there, `terminal_write_rpcs_test.go:55`) with a registry file; the earlier claim that nothing consumes `AlreadyExisted` was wrong: the web UI does (`page.tsx:605` bulk duplicate counter; the single-URL form ignores it); the MCP `import_github_issue` does not call this RPC and has no dedup path, so it is unchanged by decision (a human confirmation has no MCP equivalent and an agent-callable close would break "never silent"). Finished = status `done` AND (no PRs OR `ItemPRVerdict == Done`); archived and duplicate never. github.com with a token only, GHE reported with `blocked_reason`. Behind scope `reader:backlog` (owner to confirm the scope choice). The server re-evaluates finished and the issue state on the close call, so a client cannot force a close.

### Owner request E: mobile e2e (T6.3.3)

Verified `MOBILE_SPECS` feeds only `mobile-chromium` and `mobile-webkit`, that `chromium`/`chromium-dom` do not ignore it (so mobile specs also ran on desktop), that CI shards pick specs by `--list` over the three projects (`run-ci-shard.sh:17`, no explicit mobile spec list anywhere), that nightly already runs `mobile-webkit` with `PW_WEBKIT=1`, and that a spec absent from the baseline is strict. Changes: T6.3.3 extends `MOBILE_SPECS` with the five mobile specs, adds them to the `testIgnore` of `chromium` and `chromium-dom`, adds a registration guard test and a precondition test per spec (coarse pointer, touch points); the mobile assertions moved to `multi-pr-session-mobile`, `artifacts-tab-mobile`, `artifacts-view-mobile`, `omnibar-find-pr-mobile` and `backlog-import-close-issue-mobile` (authored in T6.3.2, T8.2.2, T10.1.3, T9.2.2, T5.5.4, each now depending on T6.3.3); validation re-points UX-S1-4, S2-5, S3-7, S4-1, S7-6, S8-4, S8-8, X-3, X-4 and adds M-1..M-7. No CI file needed an edit.
